([Pro-GenAI/awesome-checklists](https://github.com/Pro-GenAI/awesome-checklists/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/maintenance-backlog.md) · ★2)

([camercu/dotfiles](https://github.com/camercu/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/common/.config/agents/maintenance-first.md) · ★2)

- **mao-architect**: Decomposes complex problems into atomic tasks with a dependency DAG and complexity scoring. Examples: &lt;example&gt; user: "Implement JWT auth with refresh tokens for the API" assistant: "This needs decomposition. Let me use the architect agent to create a task DAG." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: "Refactor the data pipeline to support streaming" assistant: "Complex refactoring — invoki… ([aiconnai/primer](https://github.com/aiconnai/primer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mao-architect.md) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **mao-explorer**: Generates alternative solutions when a task has failed after model escalation. Spawns parallel approaches to explore the solution space, then the reviewer picks the best one. Use only as a last resort for high-complexity tasks (score ≥ 8) that failed the normal retry/escalation chain. &lt;example&gt; user: "The concurrency handler keeps failing, try different approaches" assistant: "Invoking the explorer to search for alternative solutions in parallel." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/primer](https://github.com/aiconnai/primer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mao-explorer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **mao-implementer**: Implements features, business logic, and medium-to-high complexity tasks with production quality. Handles multi-file changes, refactoring, integration code, and any task scoring 4-7 on complexity. Used automatically by the orchestrator for sonnet-tier tasks. Strictly follows Test-Driven Development with the context whiteboard and patch-based editing. &lt;example&gt; user: "Implement the authentication middleware" assistant: "This is a medium-complexity implementation task. - **mao-orchestrator**: Coordinates multi-agent execution plans. Validates task DAGs, manages git worktrees, spawns executors, monitors progress, handles failures, and merges results.
